# Star Amulet/Probability

The tables below give the percentage of a certain stat having a specific strength value, at as many quality milestones as possible. Due to the size of the tables affecting readability, it has been moved to a separate subpage. 

## Bronze Star Amulet[[](https://auth.fandom.com/signin?redirect=https%3A%2F%2Fbee-swarm-simulator.fandom.com%2Fwiki%2FStar_Amulet%2FProbability%3Fveaction%3Dedit%26section%3D1&uselang=en&metadata=article-registration-edit-article-section "Sign in to edit")]

_Note that the precise percentages may be off due to floating point errors._
